By modeling the network's rollout as a discrete-time dynamical system, TQS characterizes how quantization-induced errors propagate and amplify over the rollout horizon. Unlike conventional PTQ methods, where sensitivity analysis is often coupled to the quantization procedure, TQS enables a priori sensitivity estimation decoupled from quantizer selection and bit-width assignment. This separation allows for quantization budget planning even for black-box or compiled networks with fused operators. Building on this, we present TQS-PTQ, a flexible mixed-precision framework that requires no calibration data or costly second-order approximations. Our experiments show that a dynamical-systems perspective provides a robust, high-performing pathway for low-precision deployment in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [101] [Rarity-Gated Context Conditioning for Offline Imitation Learning-Based Maritime Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13311)
*Yongmin Kim,ByeongHoon Jeon,Sungil Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RGFiLM模块，通过稀有度门控机制调节上下文调制强度，在上下文分布不平衡的场景下减少异常检测中的误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际上下文异常检测中，上下文分布高度不平衡，导致模型在稀有上下文中产生不稳定的决策和过多误报。

Method: RGFiLM：结合特征向线性调制（FiLM）与数据驱动的稀有度得分门控，稀有上下文下门控更果断，常见上下文下门控更保守。

Result: 在船舶轨迹异常检测任务中，RGFiLM在F1与假正率权衡上优于上下文无关和上下文条件方法。

Conclusion: 显式考虑上下文稀有度是降低上下文敏感异常检测中误报的有效方法。

Abstract: Contextual anomaly detection aims to identify abnormal behavior conditional on context variables, but practical deployments often face highly imbalanced context distributions where rare regimes can be critical information. Under such frequency bias, context-conditioned models can produce unstable decisions and excessive false alarms in rare contexts. We propose Rarity-Gated Feature-wise Linear Modulation (RGFiLM), a rarity-aware conditioning module that combines feature-wise modulation (i.e., context-conditioned scaling and shifting of hidden features) with a gate controlled by a data-driven rarity score. The rarity score is estimated from the empirical distribution of context variables and regulates how strongly context modulates intermediate representations: the gate becomes more decisive under rare contexts while remaining conservative under frequent contexts. We evaluate RGFiLM on maritime trajectory anomaly detection using AIS motion sequences with ERA5 environmental context in an environment-sensitive detour scenario. When instantiated in a sequential anomaly scoring pipeline, RGFiLM achieves the best mean F1--False Positive Rate (FPR) trade-off among the compared context-agnostic and context-conditioned methods. These results suggest that explicitly accounting for context rarity is an effective approach for reducing false alarms in context-sensitive anomaly detection.

</details>


### [102] [Navigating the Safety-Fidelity Trade-off: Massive-Variate Time Series Forecasting for Power Systems via Probabilistic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13338)
*Kaijie Xu,Anqi Wang,Xilin Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PowerPhase基准测试（涵盖多达36,964个通道的电力系统概率预测）和PowerForge模型（在安全性与保真度之间取得最佳平衡）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测基准在通道规模（最多2000通道）和电力系统约束评估方面存在不足，缺乏大规模、带物理约束的基准。

Method: 构建了基于6个交流潮流网格的PowerPhase基准（2000-36964通道），提出约束感知指标；设计了PowerForge模型，包含类型特定的解码头和变量组间的因果桥。

Result: 在8个基线和3个种子下，准确性与约束满足度排序不同（安全-保真度权衡）；PowerForge在所有网格上取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: 大规模电力系统概率预测需要在分布准确性和约束满足之间取舍，PowerForge有效平衡了二者。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting models are increasingly deployed on multivariate systems with distinct channel physics and operational constraints, but existing benchmarks evaluate neither property at scale. Public canonical multivariate benchmarks cap out at 2,000 channels, while power-system benchmarks either lack temporal structure or probabilistic evaluation. We introduce PowerPhase, a probabilistic forecasting benchmark built on six transmission grids ranging from 2,000 to 36,964 jointly forecasted channels, more than an order of magnitude beyond popular canonical multivariate benchmarks. Each target trajectory is the output of an AC power-flow solve, and PowerPhase ships with constraint-aware metrics, including Safety_mBrier, NECV, and CVaR-alpha, that complement CRPS and Distortion. Across eight baselines and three seeds, distributional accuracy and constraint satisfaction rank models differently, a trade-off we term safety-fidelity. We further propose PowerForge, a scenario-based quantile forecaster with type-specific decoding heads and a causal bridge between variable groups, which achieves the best average rank on every grid.

</details>


### [103] [Enhanced Low-Density Region Exploration in Classifier-Guided Diffusion Models Through Modified Reverse Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13347)
*Jagriti Singh,Shekhar Verma,Muneendra Ojha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需额外训练的采样时密度感知引导方法，通过修改分类器梯度引导扩散模型向低置信区域探索并预测真实图像，在ImageNet上提升ADM模型召回率且保持FID相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分类器引导倾向于集中概率质量在高密度类别均值周围，导致对类条件分布尾部稀有样本的覆盖不足。现有尾部采样方法需要额外训练网络，而现有采样器和蒸馏技术未明确解决长尾覆盖问题。

Method: 基于预训练的条件扩散模型和分类器，在采样时修改引导反向动力学：1）通过修改的分类器梯度将轨迹引向低置信区域（低密度探索）；2）每一步引导采样过程向预测的真实图像靠近（保持流形真实度）。两种引导均在含噪图像上应用，而非预测噪声。

Result: 在64x64分辨率上，该采样器一致提升ADM模型召回率，同时保持可比的FID；在256x256分辨率上展示了不同引导组合的视觉效果，标准ADM分类器引导结合预测真实图像引导有助于生成高感知质量样本。

Conclusion: 提出的密度感知引导方法能在不增加训练成本的情况下有效改善扩散模型对低密度区域的覆盖，兼顾了探索低概率样本与生成高质量图像。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as state-of-the-art generative models for high-fidelity image synthesis, particularly in their classifier-free guided and classifier-guided forms. However, standard classifier guidance concentrates probability mass around high-density class mean, leading to poor coverage of rare samples in the tails of the class-conditional distributions. Recent work on diffusion-based tail sampling mitigates this by training an additional low-density-seeking classifier with a synthetic-vs-real discriminator, at the cost of additional networks and training. In parallel, a number of samplers and distillation techniques accelerate or refine diffusion sampling, but do not explicitly address long-tail coverage. We propose a purely sampling-time, density-aware extension of classifier-guided conditional diffusion model that targets low-density regions without any additional training. We have applied guidance at noisy images not on predicted noise like most diffusion models. Starting from a pretrained conditional diffusion model and classifier on ImageNet, we modify the guided reverse dynamics by steering trajectories toward low-confidence regions via the modified classifier gradient, and at each time step, we also guide the sampling process toward the predicted real image. 1st guidance helps explore low-probability samples, and 2nd guidance helps to generate samples to be close to the real data manifold. The proposed sampler consistently improves ADM model recall at 64x64 resolution while maintaining a comparable FID, and with a 256x256 ADM model, we showed the results visually with different combinations of both guidance. We also showed that standard ADM classifier guidance, combined with predicted real image guidance, helps generate high perceptual quality samples with a 256x256 ADM model on ImageNet.

</details>


### [104] [VideoMDM: Towards 3D Human Motion Generation From 2D Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13364)
*Amir Mann,Gal Michael Harari,Merav Keidar,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VideoMDM是一种无需3D真值，仅从单目视频中的2D姿态学习3D人体运动先验的扩散模型，在多个数据集上取得了接近全监督方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D人体运动生成模型依赖昂贵的3D真值数据，而单目视频数据丰富但仅有2D标注。如何利用大量易获取的2D视频数据来学习高质量的3D运动先验是核心问题。

Method: 1) 使用预训练的2D-to-3D提升器提供近似3D序列作为噪声教师；2) 在3D空间进行扩散和去噪，通过重投影到2D并与精确2D关键点比较来进行监督；3) 提出深度加权的2D重投影损失，理论上等价于直接3D监督；4) 将速度一致性和过参数化表示对齐等3D正则化器适配到2D场景。

Result: 在HumanML3D上，FID为0.88，接近完全3D监督的MDM（0.54）；在真实视频数据集Fit3D和NBA上，生成的运动在人工偏好评估中表现优异。

Conclusion: VideoMDM证明了仅从2D视频数据中学习3D运动先验的可行性，弥合了2D监督与3D监督之间的差距，具有广泛的实际应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce VideoMDM, a diffusion-based framework that trains 3D human motion priors directly from accurate 2D poses extracted from monocular videos, without any 3D ground truth. A pretrained 2D-to-3D lifter provides approximate 3D pose sequences that serve as a noisy teacher: these are diffused, denoised by the model in 3D, and supervised in 2D by reprojecting the prediction and comparing against accurate keypoints. We show that, under mild assumptions, a depth-weighted 2D reprojection loss is equivalent in expectation to direct 3D supervision, and we adapt standard 3D motion regularizers - velocity consistency and over-parameterized representation alignment - to this 2D setting. Unlike methods that lift 2D to 3D only at inference, VideoMDM learns a coherent 3D motion manifold during training. On HumanML3D it nearly closes the gap to fully 3D-supervised MDM (FID 0.88 vs 0.54); On real video datasets Fit3D and NBA the method learns to generate motions consistently preferred by humans, with strong quantitative results.

</details>


### [105] [Positional Encoding in the Context of Memristor-Based Analog Computation for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13379)
*Benedikt Hilmes,Nick Rossenbach,Ralf Schlüter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对忆阻器计算中位置编码导致模数转换（ADC）精度下降的问题，通过调整特定层的权重和ADC精度位，将计算退化降低约50%，并在无法修改ADC的情况下通过移除编码相关的线性变换降低约30%的退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻器为NLP神经模型提供高效计算，但存在权重编程和执行失真问题，尤其是位置编码的大输出值导致ADC退化严重。

Method: 调整忆阻器特定层的权重比例和ADC精度位，以及在ADC不可修改时移除编码相关的线性变换。

Result: 可修改ADC时退化降低约50%；不可修改时退化降低约30%，且能耗基本稳定。

Conclusion: 通过针对性调整ADC配置或移除特定变换，能有效缓解忆阻器计算中位置编码引起的执行退化问题。

Abstract: Memristors provide a new chance for resource-efficient computation of neural models for natural language processing by enabling analog execution of vector-matrix-multiplication. Yet, computations on these devices are currently subject to larger distortion, both in weight programming and execution. In this work, we identify large output values of transformed positional encodings to cause major degradation within analog-to-digital conversion (ADC) as part of memristor-based computation. By adjusting the proportion of weight and precision bits of the ADC of specific memristor layers, we reduce the degradation of the execution by ~50% relative, while keeping the estimated energy consumption stable. Additionally, we investigate scenarios where the ADC cannot be modified. In that case the degradation can be reduced by ~30% relative after removing encoding-related linear transformations.

</details>


### [106] [Hölder++: Improving the Quality-Coherence Trade-off in Multimodal VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13381)
*Huyen Vo,María Martínez-García,Isabel Valera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Hölder++模型，通过精确Hölder池化、共享/私有表示分离和层次化推理，改善了多模态VAE在生成质量与语义一致性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态VAE方法在生成质量与跨模态语义一致性之间存在权衡问题，难以同时生成高质量且语义一致的样本。

Method: (i)首次在多模态VAE中实现无近似的Hölder池化；(ii)扩展架构以建模共享表示和私有（模态特有）表示（Hölder+）；(iii)引入层次化推理进一步增强共享与私有表示的解耦（Hölder++）。

Result: Hölder++在生成质量-一致性权衡上持续改进，产生更有结构的隐空间，学习到的共享表示对下游任务具有信息量。

Conclusion: Hölder++通过精确池化、共享/私有分离及层次化推理，有效提升多模态VAE的生成质量与语义一致性。

Abstract: Existing approaches for multimodal variational autoencoders (VAEs) face a trade-off between generative quality and coherence-i.e., they struggle to generate realistic and diverse samples that, at the same time, are semantically consistent across modalities. A recent work shows that using a simple approximation to Hölder pooling as an aggregation method improves coherence over the SOTA MMVAE+, despite assuming a single shared representation across all modalities. Yet, it slightly compromises sample diversity. Inspired by this insight, we propose Hölder++, a novel multimodal VAE that improves the generative quality-coherence trade-off through: (i) the first implementation of Hölder pooling without any approximation for multimodal VAEs; (ii) an extended architecture that models distinct shared and private (i.e., modality-specific) representations (Hölder+); and (iii) hierarchical inference that further enhances the disentanglement between the shared and private representations (Hölder++). Our experiments corroborate that Hölder++ consistently improves the generative quality-coherence trade-off, yields more structured latent spaces, and learns shared representations that are informative for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [107] [PolyFlow: Safe and Efficient Polytope-Constrained Flow Matching with Constraint Embedding and Projection-free Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13400)
*Jianming Ma,Qiyue Yang,Yang Zhang,Liyun Yan,Zhanxiang Cao,Yazhou Zhang,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyFlow提出了一种多面体约束流匹配框架，通过将约束直接嵌入模型和流动力学中，在保证零约束违反的同时保持高分布保真度，显著降低了推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的生成模型在安全关键物理系统中难以部署，因为事后修正方法计算开销大且可能扭曲学习到的分布。

Method: PolyFlow采用离散时间流公式和无投影架构，直接将任意多面体约束嵌入模型和流动力学中，消除了离散化误差，无需昂贵的迭代求解器。

Result: 在多个规划与控制任务中实现零约束违反，同时保持高分布保真度；相比基线方法显著降低推理延迟，在安全、效率和生成质量之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: PolyFlow为安全关键物理系统中流式生成模型的部署提供了一种高效、可靠的约束满足方案。

Abstract: While flow-based generative models have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of domains, deploying them in safety-critical physical systems remains challenging due to strict constraint requirements. Existing approaches typically enforce safety through post-hoc corrections, which incur substantial computational overhead and may distort the learned distribution. We propose PolyFlow, a polytope-constrained flow matching framework that embeds constraints directly into the model and flow dynamics. PolyFlow introduces a discrete-time flow formulation and a projection-free architecture, which eliminate the discretization error and guarantee strict satisfaction of arbitrary polyhedral constraints, without the need for expensive iterative solvers. Experimental results show that PolyFlow achieves zero constraint violation while maintaining high distributional fidelity across a range of planning and control tasks. Compared to state-of-the-art constrained generation baselines, PolyFlow significantly reduces inference latency and demonstrates a favorable trade-off between safety, efficiency, and generative quality. Code is available on https://github.com/MJianM/PolyFlow.

</details>


### [108] [How Much Memory Do We Need? Adaptive Memory Gate for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13443)
*Jihyeon Hur,Yongseok Kwon,Min-Gi Jo,Jeongwhan Choi,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AMGFNO，通过可学习门控动态调整记忆权重，解决固定记忆权重在低分辨率PDE求解中适应性不足的问题，在K-S和Burgers方程上实现55-79%的nRMSE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强神经算子在不同观测条件（如分辨率、物理参数）下使用固定记忆权重，限制了模型适应性。实验表明最优记忆权重随分辨率和粘度变化，固定权重无法在所有场景下取得最优性能。

Method: 提出AMGFNO（Adaptive Memory Gated Factorized Neural Operator），引入可学习门控机制动态调制记忆权重，使模型能根据输入条件自适应调整记忆强度。

Result: 在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky和Burgers方程的低分辨率设定下，相比基线方法实现55-79%的nRMSE降低；学习到的门控值随分辨率增加从约0.7自动下降至接近零。

Conclusion: 自适应记忆门控机制能有效提升神经算子在多分辨率场景下的泛化性能，验证了动态调整记忆权重的必要性。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for solving time-dependent PDEs. Among recent advances, memory-augmented neural operators explicitly incorporate past states and have achieved remarkable performance under low-resolution observation settings. However, existing approaches apply a fixed memory weight regardless of observation conditions, such as resolution or physical parameters, limiting their adaptability. Our preliminary experiments reveal that optimal memory weight varies with resolution and viscosity, implying that a fixed memory weight cannot simultaneously optimize performance across diverse settings. We propose AMGFNO, which dynamically modulates memory weight through a learnable gate. On the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky and Burgers' equations, AMGFNO achieves 55-79% nRMSE reduction over at low resolution, with the learned gate value automatically decreasing from $\bar{g} \approx 0.7$ to near-zero as resolution increases.

</details>


### [109] [Clustering Node Attributed Networks with Graph Neural Networks and Self Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13444)
*Rodrigo de Sapienza Luna,Daniel Ratton Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的无监督图聚类框架，通过多轮自学习迭代改进节点表示和聚类结果，在合成数据和真实数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的图聚类方法仅利用网络结构（边）或节点属性信息，而现实数据中两者均包含有用信息。现有方法未能充分利用两者协同作用，且缺乏有效的无监督学习机制。

Method: 提出一个多轮自学习框架：每轮中使用GNN生成节点表示并进行聚类，聚类结果影响下一轮的图结构；同时每轮构建一个上下文图来辅助节点表示生成，整个过程无需监督信号。

Result: 在合成数据上，该方法能同时从网络边和节点属性中提取信息，当两者都不够强时优于仅利用单一信息的方法；多轮学习持续提升性能，优于单轮训练；在真实数据集上，当聚类大小均衡时与现有方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 该无监督GNN聚类框架通过多轮自学习有效融合网络结构和节点属性信息，性能优于传统单轮方法，且在平衡聚类场景下具有竞争力。

Abstract: Graph clustering - partitioning the node set of a graph into disjoint subsets that reflect some latent information - is a fundamental problem as it finds applications in a myriad of different scenarios. While this classic problem has been tackled for decades by different communities, a recent variation of the problem driven by real data considers the scenario where nodes have attributes that are also informative. This has triggered novel methods that simultaneously leverage network information (edges) and node information (attributed) in the design of novel clustering algorithms. This work proposes a novel framework that builds on prior works that have applied graph neural networks (GNN) to graph clustering. The proposed framework operates in rounds of self learning in a fully unsupervised setting. In each round, a GNN generates representations for nodes that are used to cluster the nodes. This clustering influences the graph used to generate the node representation in the next round. Moreover, a context graph built in each round using the original graph is used to generate the node representations. Empirical results show that the proposed methodology extracts information from both network edges and node attributes in synthetic data, outperforming algorithms focused solely on the network or attributes when neither are very informative. Multiple rounds of learning also improve the performance and always outperforms a long single round of training (i.e., classic GNN graph clustering). When considering real datasets, empirical results indicate that the proposed methodology is competitive to state-of-the-art methods when cluster sizes are balanced.

</details>


### [110] [Uncertainty Estimation for Molecular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13451)
*Paul Seij,Christian A. Naesseth,Stephan Mandt,Metod Jazbec*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于预训练分子扩散模型的后验不确定性估计方法，通过拉普拉斯近似度量生成轨迹中噪声预测的变异性，该分数与样本质量负相关，可用于过滤低质量样本以提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在3D分子生成中广泛应用，但缺乏对生成分子质量低下的可靠指示信号。

Method: 基于去噪网络的拉普拉斯近似，度量整个生成轨迹中噪声预测的变异性，得到每个样本的不确定性分数。

Result: 不确定性分数与样本质量指标呈负相关，能有效区分高质量与低质量分子。

Conclusion: 所提出的不确定性分数可用于过滤生成样本，通过测试时缩放提升模型整体性能。

Abstract: Diffusion models have seen wide adoption for 3D molecular generation, yet they offer no principled signal of when a generated molecule is likely to be of low quality. We propose a post-hoc method for estimating per-sample uncertainty in pretrained molecular diffusion models. Building on a Laplace approximation of the denoising network, we measure the variability of the noise prediction across the generation trajectory. Empirically, we show that the resulting uncertainty score is informative of sample quality, exhibiting a negative correlation with established sample-level quality metrics. We further study how the proposed uncertainty score can be used to filter generated samples, improving model performance via test-time scaling.

</details>


### [111] [Reinforcement Learning for Neural Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13461)
*Shaivi Malik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将神经网络模型编辑转化为强化学习问题的探索性框架，通过MaskWorld和ShiftWorld两种环境让智能体基于奖励信号学习编辑策略，并在偏见缓解和机器遗忘任务上取得良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计针对特定目标的神经网络编辑算法通常耗时耗力，需要专门定制的算法，缺乏通用性。

Method: 将模型编辑形式化为强化学习问题，提出两个环境：MaskWorld（乘法缩放权重）和ShiftWorld（加法更新权重）。奖励函数结合了效用保持目标和任务特定编辑目标。

Result: 在机器遗忘任务中，遗忘集准确率降至接近0%，同时保留集准确率保持90%以上；在偏见缓解任务中，偏见相关性能提升超过5%，同时保持分类效用。

Conclusion: 神经网络模型编辑可以转化为强化学习问题，使编辑策略能够从奖励反馈中学习，而无需为每个任务手动设计算法。

Abstract: Editing pretrained neural networks requires specialized algorithms tailored to specific objectives. Designing such algorithms is often time-consuming and demands significant effort. We present an exploratory framework that formulates neural model editing as a reinforcement learning problem, where agents modify models using reward feedback. We introduce two environments: MaskWorld, where agents scale weights multiplicatively, and ShiftWorld, where agents apply additive weight updates. The reward function combines a utility-preservation objective with a task-specific editing objective, enabling agents to learn targeted modifications while maintaining overall model performance. We evaluate the framework on bias mitigation in text classification and machine unlearning in image classification, both of which traditionally rely on specialized algorithms. Our results show that the learned policies reduce forget set accuracy to nearly 0% while preserving over 90% retain set accuracy on the unlearning task. In the bias mitigation setting, the learned policies improve bias-related performance by more than 5% while maintaining general classification utility. Our findings show that neural model editing can be cast as a reinforcement learning problem, allowing editing policies to be learned from reward feedback rather than manually engineered for each task.

</details>


### [112] [MaxProof: Scaling Mathematical Proof with Generative-Verifier RL and Population-Level Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13473)
*Jiacheng Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yanmohan Wang,Lin Li,Tiancheng Qin,Qin Wang,Zhengmao Zhu,Tianle Li,Jingyang Li,Zehan Li,Binyang Jiang,Jin Zhu,Han Ding,Fei Yu,Chenyu Du,Zijian Song,Jiayuan Song,Zhi Zhang,Yunan Huang,Weiyu Cheng,Pengyu Zhao,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MaxProof是一种面向竞赛级数学证明的群体级测试时扩展框架，通过结合证明生成、验证和修正能力，在IMO 2025和USAMO 2026上超过人类金牌阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在竞赛级数学证明任务上表现有限，缺乏可靠的证明验证和修正机制，且测试时扩展方法多局限于单样本推理，未充分利用群体候选证明的潜力。

Method: 首先训练三个面向证明的能力：证明生成、证明验证、基于批评的证明修正，采用低假阳性率的纵深防御生成验证器。在测试时，将M3模型作为生成器、验证器、精炼器和排序器，对候选证明群体进行搜索，通过锦标赛选择返回最终证明。

Result: M3模型在IMO 2025上达到35/42，在USAMO 2026上达到36/42，均超过人类金牌阈值。

Conclusion: MaxProof通过群体级测试时扩展，有效提升了竞赛级数学证明的性能，达到了超越人类金牌水平的表现。

Abstract: We present MaxProof, a population-level test-time scaling framework for competition-level mathematical proof in the MiniMax-M3 series. M3 first trains three proof-oriented capabilities -- proof generation, proof verification, and critique-conditioned proof repair -- using a defense-in-depth generative verifier engineered for low false-positive rate. These capabilities are merged into a single released M3 model. At test time, MaxProof treats the model as a generator, verifier, refiner, and ranker, searches over a population of candidate proofs, and returns one final proof through tournament selection. With MaxProof test-time scaling, the M3 model reaches 35/42 on IMO 2025 and 36/42 on USAMO 2026, exceeding the human gold-medal threshold on both.

</details>


### [113] [SupraBench: A Benchmark for Supramolecular Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13477)
*Tianyi Ma,Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Weixiang Sun,Ziming Li,Connor R. Schmidt,Chuxu Zhang,Matthew J. Webber,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个超分子化学基准SupraBench，包含四个核心推理任务和一个视觉辅助任务，并发布了16M token的领域语料SupraPMC。实验表明，现有LLMs在所有任务上表现不佳，领域自适应预训练对分布内回归任务有帮助，但会牺牲严格的格式输出要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前设计主客体系统耗时且需大量人工验证，虽然LLMs在分子结合任务上表现强劲，但缺乏系统评估LLMs在超分子化学推理能力的基准。

Method: 与领域专家合作设计了四个基础任务（结合亲和力预测、最佳结合物选择、溶剂识别、主客体描述）和一个基于视觉的分子识别任务；同时从Europe PMC中蒸馏出16M token的SupraPMC语料库用于领域自适应预训练。

Result: 开源和闭源LLMs在所有任务上均存在显著的提升空间；领域自适应预训练在分布内回归任务上有效，但与严格格式输出之间存在权衡；不同任务家族的难度分布差异明显，揭示了当前超分子化学推理中的具体缺陷。

Conclusion: 现有LLMs在超分子化学推理方面仍有很大改进空间，SupraBench为未来LLMs在化学领域的评估提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: Supramolecular chemistry, which includes the study of non-covalent host-guest assemblies, has advanced various applications. However, designing host-guest systems remains time-consuming, requiring days of dry-lab verification per candidate pair. Although LLMs have emerged as a fast alternative with strong performance on molecular binding tasks, no benchmark currently systematically evaluates LLMs for host-guest reasoning across fundamental supramolecular chemistry tasks, e.g., binding affinity prediction. To this end, we collaborate with domain experts to release the first Supramolecular Benchmark, called SupraBench, to evaluate LLMs in chemistry reasoning. Specifically, we design four fundamental tasks, i.e., binding affinity prediction, top-binder selection, solvent identification, and host-guest description, plus an auxiliary vision-based task for molecular identification. We also release SupraPMC, a curated 16M-token corpus of Supramolecular chemistry articles distilled from Europe PMC, to support the adaptation to the supramolecular domain. We benchmark a broad range of open and proprietary LLMs and find that LLMs leave substantial headroom across all tasks. Domain adaptation pretraining over SupraPMC transfers cleanly to in-distribution regression but trades off against strict letter-format output. Moreover, the difficulty profile differs sharply across task families, revealing distinct failure modes that indicate specific gaps in current supramolecular chemistry reasoning. Our source codes and benchmark datasets are available at https://github.com/Tianyi-Billy-Ma/SupraBench.

</details>


### [114] [CRAFTIIF: Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13486)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFTIIF是一个全无监督的多变量时间序列异常检测框架，通过K=500个跨四种小波族的随机特征提取和五个结构化孤立森林，统一检测点、分布、时间和集体四种异常类型，在mTSBench基准上排名第一（VUS-PR提升40.7%），并提供直接异常类型归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督方法大多只针对一两种异常类型（点异常、分布异常、时间异常、集体异常），且缺乏可解释性，无法统一处理多变量时间序列中四种结构不同的异常。

Method: 生成K=500个跨四种小波族（Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet）的随机解析小波特征，每种小波针对特定异常类型；构建五个结构化孤立森林（四种类型各一个+一个元IF处理复合异常）；采用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值自动校准检测率。

Result: 在mTSBench基准的19个数据集上平均F1=0.228（全部）/0.322（13个可检测数据集），VUS-PR达0.463（较此前最佳0.329提升40.7%），排名第一。消融实验证实自适应阈值（+38%）、四分支结构（+20%）、元IF（+23%）均不可或缺。

Conclusion: CRAFTIIF通过跨分辨率解析小波特征与结构化孤立森林，首次实现了无需数据集特定调参的无监督四类型异常检测，性能显著超越现有方法，且通过分支触发机制提供内建可解释性。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is challenged by four structurally distinct anomaly types -- point (isolated spikes), distributional (level shifts), temporal (rhythm changes), and collective (inter-sensor correlation breakdowns) -- each requiring different feature representations. Most unsupervised methods target only one or two types and provide limited interpretability. We present CRAFTIIF (Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest), a fully unsupervised framework targeting all four types without dataset-specific tuning. CRAFTIIF generates K=500 random analytic wavelet feature draws across four families (Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet), each targeting a specific anomaly type, feeding five structured Isolation Forests -- one per type plus a meta-IF for compound anomalies. An adaptive Otsu/MAD threshold calibrates detection automatically across anomaly rates from 0.1% to 69.2%. Because each IF is trained exclusively on type-specific features, branch firing provides direct anomaly-type attribution by construction, without post-hoc explanation. Evaluated on all 19 datasets of the mTSBench benchmark (Zhou et al., TMLR 2026), CRAFTIIF achieves mean F1=0.228 (all 19 datasets) and F1=0.322 (13 detectable datasets), ranking first among all 25 evaluated methods on VUS-PR (0.463 vs. previous best 0.329, +40.7%). A diagnostic framework -- oracle F1, detectability limits, and branch separation ratios -- identifies 6 of 19 datasets as fundamentally undetectable by any unsupervised method. Ablation over 11 conditions confirms adaptive thresholding (+38% F1), four-branch structure (+20%), and meta-IF (+23%) are each essential. Code: https://github.com/smitswil/craftiif

</details>


### [115] [A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13565)
*Sophia Tang,Yuchen Zhu,Molei Tao,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出A2D2框架，通过联合优化插入和去掩码策略以及基于质量的推理调度，实现任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调，并理论上保证收敛到奖励倾斜分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型虽支持任意长度序列生成，但缺乏原则性的奖励引导微调方法。

Method: 推导联合插入-去掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，提出自适应联合解码(AJD)损失，联合优化插入策略、去掩码策略和推理调度。

Result: A2D2在奖励优化、生成灵活性和准确性上优于固定长度微调和推理时引导方法。

Conclusion: A2D2为任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调提供了统一且理论保证的框架。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.

</details>


### [116] [Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13568)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了调整杯积神经层（adjusted cup product neural layer），通过硬编码杯积与高阶规范理论的调整项，实现规范不变的神经网络读出头。主要理论结果表明，在闭链上输出完全取决于调整系数，该系数是规范不变信号的唯一来源，且该可观测量是非零二次型，对一重和二重规范变换严格不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学和几何学中许多重要的可观测量都是上链的杯积（cup product），但传统方法难以保证神经网络的规范不变性，因此在处理带有规范对称性的物理或几何问题时存在局限。

Method: 提出调整杯积神经层，将杯积与来自高阶规范理论的调整项硬编码到神经网络中，构建了一个天然具有规范不变性的读出头。

Result: 在闭链上，网络的输出完全由调整系数决定；若将该系数设为零，则输出完全消失，表明调整是规范不变信号的唯一来源。该可观测量被证明是非零二次型，且对一重和二重规范变换严格不变。

Conclusion: 该工作为在神经网络中嵌入物理和几何中的规范对称性提供了一种新方法，使得模型能够自动满足规范不变性，而不需要依赖数据学习或额外的约束。

Abstract: Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.

</details>


### [117] [Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13571)
*Yifan Hu,Hongzhou Chen,Peiyuan Liu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Timeflies是一个统一框架，将时间序列预测重新定义为未来可观测性推断与数值估计的联合问题，通过观测流和数值流双流耦合机制，在高度不完整和不规则的真实世界时间序列预测中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理不完整和不规则时间序列时，隐式假设推理时未来有效观测的时间戳是已知的，这限制了实际应用价值。许多系统中更根本的问题不仅是未来值是什么，还包括是否会发生有效观测。

Method: 提出Timeflies统一框架，包含观测流和数值流两个流，通过三个专用模块（可靠性感知嵌入、观测引导的依赖建模、联合预测）进行耦合。同时构建Shadow基准数据集，引入观测-数值联合熵（OVJE）评估指标。

Result: 大量实验表明Timeflies在性能上一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 显式建模未来可观测性对于处理缺失值的时间序列预测至关重要。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.

</details>


### [118] [Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13589)
*Meher Sai Preetam,Meher Bhaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB)的数学严格框架，用于对基于bootstrap的装袋集成进行后训练压缩和概率校准。通过在概率单纯形上最小化袋外损失，并引入凹二次惩罚解决"L1-单纯形悖论"，实现高达96%的集成压缩，同时保持或提升泛化精度和校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准装袋集成（如随机森林、Bagged SVM、Bagged神经网络）对所有基估计器赋予均匀投票权重，忽略了各基估计器在不同局部区域的差异化能力，且导致模型过度自信。现有方法缺乏对集成压缩和概率校准的联合优化。

Method: 将集成剪枝与校准形式化为概率单纯形上的联合优化问题，通过最小化袋外(OOB)损失来求解。针对L1范数在单纯形上为常数、无法实现剪枝的"L1-单纯形悖论"，引入凹二次惩罚项诱导稀疏性。该方法与模型无关。

Result: 可实现高达96%的集成压缩率，带来线性的推理加速，并能显著降低期望校准误差(ECE)，同时保持或提升泛化准确性。

Conclusion: SCSB是一种模型无关、数学严谨的后训练框架，能有效解决装袋集成的过自信和冗余问题，在压缩、校准和精度三个维度同时取得优异表现。

Abstract: We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.

</details>


### [119] [Beyond the Commitment Boundary: Probing Epiphenomenal Chain-of-Thought in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13603)
*Daniel Scalena,Sara Candussio,Luca Bortolussi,Elisabetta Fersini,Malvina Nissim,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现链式推理过程中存在一个"承诺边界"，即模型在某个单一推理步骤中突然从临时猜测转变为稳定高置信度的最终答案，后续推理步骤对答案概率无实际影响；利用这一现象，可在承诺边界提前退出，平均缩短55%的推理长度且几乎不影响性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前链式推理虽已成为推理时扩展的主流范式，但推理步骤对最终答案的因果影响尚不明确。

Method: 通过早期退出估计每一步的因果重要性，利用注意力探针从中间步骤线性解码答案形成阶段，并基于承诺边界信号实现早期退出。

Result: 推理通常在一个步骤内完成从临时猜测到稳定答案的跃迁，后续步骤是"附带现象"；注意力探针可高精度解码答案形成阶段并泛化到未见任务；早期退出平均缩短55%推理长度，性能损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 链式推理中存在可预测的承诺边界，利用该信号可显著压缩推理成本而不损害性能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is the dominant paradigm for inference-time scaling in language models, yet the causal influence of individual steps on the final answer poorly understood. We estimate each step's causal importance via early exit and use this measure to study how answers form across the reasoning traces of several model families. Across diverse tasks, we find that reasoning typically crosses a \emph{commitment boundary} -- a sharp transition from transient intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer. This transition often happens in a single step, well before the model's reasoning block ends, and is followed by \emph{epiphenomenal} CoT steps that leave the final answer probability unaltered. Using attention probes, we show that answer-formation stages can be linearly decoded from intermediate reasoning steps with high accuracy and generalize robustly to unseen reasoning tasks. We exploit this signal to early-exit reasoning blocks at the commitment boundary, reducing the length of CoTs up to 55\% on average with negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [120] [The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13637)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Recovery Subspace Dimensionality等指标发现，持续学习中的灾难性遗忘并非信息破坏，而是可访问性和流形对齐问题，遗忘的知识仍可被紧凑解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"灾难性遗忘即知识破坏"的传统观点，基于Accessibility Collapse框架探究持续学习中可恢复性的几何结构。

Method: 在Split CIFAR-100上使用ResNet-18进行十任务序列训练，引入Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t)度量，分析可恢复性、表征漂移和恢复复杂度。

Result: 恢复维度保持稳定（平均k_t=8.0），主角度漂移与可恢复性强相关（r=-0.862），几何模型解释82.2%的方差。

Conclusion: 支持Stable Recovery Manifold假说，灾难性遗忘本质是可访问性和流形对齐问题，而非信息破坏。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.

</details>


### [121] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了On-policy Distillation (OPD)的更新特性，发现其具有稀疏性和谱集中性，且密集教师监督并未导致密集的参数重写，而是保留了重要的几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD结合了on-policy学生轨迹和密集教师监督已成为流行的后训练方法，但其如何改变模型参数尚不清楚，因此需要深入分析其更新机制。

Method: 在多个语言和视觉-语言模型对上，从稀疏性和几何两个维度分析OPD更新的参数变化特征。

Result: OPD更新具有坐标稀疏性，分布于各层且以FFN为主；更新数值上满秩但谱集中，主要偏离源权重的奇异子空间，落在权重接近零的坐标上。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未使OPD变成普通密集参数重写，OPD保留了on-policy后训练的重要几何特征。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


### [122] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了图神经网络中位置编码的截断版本，发现不同截断位置编码的表达能力存在根本性差异，截断谱编码不再强于1-WL测试，并实验表明混合截断编码效果更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管完整版谱位置编码和基于游走的位置编码在表达能力上等价，但实际中使用的截断版本（如前k个特征向量或邻接矩阵的前k次幂）的理论性质尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过理论分析证明截断后不同位置编码家族的表达能力存在根本差异；引入k-调和距离族谱编码以展示相近截断编码间的表达能力区别；并在真实数据集上实验验证混合截断编码的优势。

Result: 截断后，谱位置编码不再强于1-WL测试；不同截断位置编码的表达能力存在根本区别；混合使用多种截断编码优于单一编码。

Conclusion: 实际中应混合使用多种截断位置编码，而非依赖单一类型，以获得更好的性能。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Identifiability Without Gaussianity: Symbolic World Models and Near-Infinite Temporal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12471)
*Seth Dobrin,Łukasz Chmiel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明，联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的线性可识别性受限于高斯过程假设，而新提出的物理基符号架构（PGSA）通过因果生成器的符号基础，实现了对所有物理体系的精确线性可识别性，并具备近乎无限的时序一致性，这是统计世界模型无法达到的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Klindt等人证明了JEPA在线性可识别性上存在高斯边界——即只有当世界潜在动态服从高斯平稳过程时才能实现线性恢复，且对于非高斯系统，统计世界模型的表示误差会随时间单调增长。本文旨在证明这一极限是统计对齐机制的人为产物，而非世界模型的固有属性。

Method: 提出物理基符号架构（PGSA），通过将模型构建于世界动态的因果生成器之上，实现符号层面的基础对齐。文章在数学上证明了PGSA的三个核心性质，并利用Lean 4和Mathlib4 v4.31.0对其中四个定理进行了形式化验证。

Result: (1) PGSA在所有物理体系下均能实现精确线性可识别性，无论潜在分布如何；(2) PGSA的每步误差仅受限于数值精度；(3) PGSA具备近乎无限的时序一致性，可在无界步数内保持时序一致性。而统计世界模型在任何非高斯系统下均无法实现该性质。

Conclusion: 符号化基础于世界动态的因果生成器，是实现近乎无限时序一致性的充分条件；在非高斯场景下，这是唯一条件。

Abstract: Klindt, LeCun, and Balestriero (arXiv:2605.26379) proved that Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) achieve linear identifiability, the linear recovery of the world's true latent variables, if and only if the world's latent dynamics follow a Gaussian, stationary process. This Gaussian boundary implies a fundamental limit on temporal consistency: for any non-Gaussian physical system, the representation error of a statistical World Model grows monotonically with time. We prove that this limit is an artifact of the statistical alignment mechanism, not a property of World Models in general. We introduce the Physics-Grounded Symbolic Architecture (PGSA) and prove three results: (1) a PGSA achieves exact linear identifiability for all physical regimes, regardless of the latent distribution; (2) the per-step error of a PGSA is bounded by numerical precision alone; and (3) as a direct consequence, a PGSA maintains temporal consistency for an unbounded number of transitions, a property we term near-infinite temporal consistency. We further prove that statistical World Models cannot achieve this property for any non-Gaussian system, regardless of model capacity or the volume of training data. The algebraic cores of four of the theorems are formalized in Lean 4 with Mathlib4 v4.31.0 (zero sorry placeholders); the Klindt et al. converse is taken as an external premise. The contrast establishes that symbolic grounding in the causal generator of the world's dynamics is the sufficient condition and, in non-Gaussian regimes, the only condition for near-infinite temporal consistency.

</details>


### [124] [Epistemic Uncertainty Is Not the Reducible Kind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12646)
*Robin Young*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了标准预测不确定性分类中，将认知不确定性定义为“可通过收集更多数据来减少的部分”与该定义常用的互信息度量在扩展性上不一致。作者提出新的三分法：偶然不确定性、样本可减少认知不确定性和机制可减少认知不确定性，并指出在分布内数据永远不会减少机制不可减少的不确定性，且通常会使其增加。集成分歧（常用的认知估计）实际追踪的是训练过程而非认知项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分类法中对认知不确定性的定义（可通过更多数据减少）与其常用度量（互信息项）存在扩展性不一致的问题，需要修正。

Method: 构造显式反例证明定义与度量不一致；提出三分法理论框架；通过理论分析和实验验证。

Result: 标准定义与度量不一致；机制不可减少的不确定性无法通过数据减少；集成分歧与认知项不一致；有限样本检验和种子扫描实验支持理论。

Conclusion: 预测不确定性分类需要重新审视，认知不确定性的可减少性依赖于具体的数据获取类别，现有度量方法存在根本性缺陷。

Abstract: The standard taxonomy of predictive uncertainty defines epistemic uncertainty as the part removable by collecting more data, while the standard measure identifies it with a mutual-information term. We prove the definition and the measure are extensionally inconsistent. On an explicit construction, the measure assigns all uncertainty to the epistemic class, yet no quantity of training data reduces it. Reducibility is instead a property of the pair (uncertainty, acquisition class), and the dichotomy resolves into three parts: aleatoric, sample-reducible epistemic, and mechanism-reducible epistemic uncertainty. An exact identity for the value of an observation shows that in-distribution data never reduces mechanism-irreducible uncertainty and generically increases it. Ensemble disagreement, the deployed epistemic estimate, tracks the training procedure rather than the epistemic term. It collapses to zero beneath a positive truth under consistent training, and equals hyperparameter-scaled initialization noise under interpolation. A finite-sample falsification test and seed-swept experiments confirm the theory.

</details>


### [125] [Prediction-Powered Causal Inference by Automatic Debiased Machine Learning and Semi-Supervised Riesz Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12892)
*Masahiro Kato*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半监督框架下的因果与结构参数半参数有效估计方法（PPCI），通过结合无标签辅助变量与高效影响函数，构建了两种基于去偏机器学习的估计量（DML-PPCI），其渐近方差可达理论效率界，并开发了半监督广义Riesz回归用于关键成分估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断仅依赖有标签数据，导致估计效率受限。现实中存在大量无标签辅助变量（如电子健康记录中的未标注协变量），如何利用这些额外信息提升因果参数估计的精度是一个重要但尚未充分解决的问题。

Method: 推导了半监督设定下的高效影响函数与效率界，证明使用辅助变量可降低渐近方差。基于去偏机器学习框架，构建了两种估计量：EE-DML-PPCI（基于估计方程）和TMLE-DML-PPCI（基于目标学习）。开发了半监督广义Riesz回归用于估计影响函数中的Riesz表示子，并保证了收敛速度。

Result: 两种DML-PPCI估计量的渐近方差均达到推导出的理论效率界；高效影响函数同时具有Neyman正交性；半监督广义Riesz回归在理论上有收敛速度保证。

Conclusion: 半监督框架能有效利用无标签辅助信息提升因果推断效率，所提方法兼具理论最优性与实践可行性，为预测驱动的因果推断提供了新范式。

Abstract: This study investigates semiparametric efficient estimation of causal and structural parameters in a semi-supervised setting. In our setting, unlabeled auxiliary regressors are available in addition to labeled observations consisting of outcomes and regressors. Our goal is to construct estimators of causal and structural parameters whose asymptotic variances are smaller than those of estimators constructed using only labeled data. We refer to this framework as prediction-powered causal inference (PPCI). We first derive the efficient influence function and the efficiency bound, which imply that the use of auxiliary regressors can attain a smaller asymptotic variance than the efficiency bound attainable from labeled observations alone. Then, by combining the efficient influence function with the debiased machine learning (DML) framework, we propose methods that we call DML-PPCI. If we construct an estimating-equation estimator, we refer to the method as EE-DML-PPCI; if we construct a targeted-learning estimator, we refer to the method as TMLE-DML-PPCI. The asymptotic variances of both estimators match our derived efficiency bound. In the construction of the estimators, estimation of the efficient influence function plays an important role. In our study, the efficient influence function is also a Neyman orthogonal score, which depends on the Riesz representer and the regression function. For Riesz representer estimation, we develop semi-supervised generalized Riesz regression with convergence rate guarantees.

</details>


### [126] [Robust State-Conditional Feature-Weighted Jump Models for Temporal Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13146)
*Federico P. Cortese,Alessio Farcomeni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种鲁棒的、特征加权的跳跃模型用于时间依赖聚类，通过Tukey双权损失函数处理异常值，并引入惩罚项平滑时间转移，在模拟和实际数据中表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间依赖聚类方法对异常值敏感，且难以在时间演变过程中为不同状态分配特征权重，因此需要一种兼具鲁棒性和特征选择能力的聚类模型。

Method: 采用Tukey双权损失函数增强鲁棒性，加入惩罚项平滑时间转移，并通过额外参数控制特征权重在不同状态间的变异性，实现状态特定的特征加权聚类。

Result: 模拟实验表明该方法能准确恢复真实聚类序列并识别相关特征，尤其在存在异常值时优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 该方法在科索沃冲突致死人数（1998-2000）和十二个欧洲国家宏观经济表现（1949-2024）两个实证应用中验证了有效性。

Abstract: We propose a robust feature-weighted jump model for time-dependent clustering. A penalty is used to encourage smoothness of transitions over time, while robustness is achieved through the use of a Tukey's biweight loss function. An additional parameter controls the variability of feature weights across states, allowing the model to assign state-specific relevance to each feature. We illustrate in simulation how the method accurately recovers the true cluster sequence and reliably identifies relevant features, outperforming competing approaches, particularly in the presence of outliers. We conclude with two empirical applications, one on the number of conflict-related homicides in Kosovo in the period 1998-2000, and another on macroeconomic performance of twelve European countries in the period 1949-2024.

</details>


### [127] [ProtoX-AD: Self-Explainable Time Series Anomaly Detection and Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13277)
*Aitor Sánchez-Ferrera,Elisabeth Wetzer,Kristoffer Wickstrøm,Michael Kampffmeyer,Robert Jenssen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出ProtoX-AD框架，通过原型学习实现自监督时间序列异常检测的可解释性，在保持检测性能的同时提供语义一致的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督时间序列异常检测方法虽然性能优异，但缺乏可解释性，无法对检测到的异常提供特征层面的洞察。

Method: ProtoX-AD是一个基于原型的自解释框架，学习变换感知的潜在表示和可解释原型，通过原型匹配实现异常检测与解释。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，ProtoX-AD实现了与黑盒方法相当的检测性能，同时提供了比现有可解释基线更一致、语义更有意义的解释。

Conclusion: ProtoX-AD成功解决了自监督TSAD的可解释性瓶颈，并支持系统分析变换设计对检测性能和可解释性的影响。

Abstract: Recent advances in time series anomaly detection (TSAD) have highlighted the effectiveness of self-supervised classification-based approaches. These methods apply transformations to normal training samples, training a classifier to recognize transformation-specific patterns that help identify anomalies through increased classification errors. Despite their strong performance, a significant challenge is their lack of explainability, as they provide limited insight into the characteristics of flagged anomalies. To address this limitation, we propose ProtoX-AD, a prototype-based self-explainable framework for self-supervised TSAD. ProtoX-AD learns transformation-aware latent representations alongside interpretable prototypes, enabling both accurate anomaly detection and the identification of distinct anomalous profiles through prototype-based explanations. Additionally, it allows for systematic analysis of how transformation design impacts detection performance and explainability. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that ProtoX-AD achieves detection performance comparable to its black-box counterparts while offering more consistent and semantically meaningful explanations than existing explainable baselines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aitorzan3/ProtoX-AD.

</details>


### [128] [Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees for Stratified Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13295)
*Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees for Stratified Classification Cristian Buoncompagni,Stefano Pellegrino,Giulia Vannucci,Roberta Siciliano*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为同步潜在预算树（SLBT）的概率机器学习框架，用于在存在分层因子（如时间、空间或人口统计变量）的情况下构建可解释的分类树。该方法通过模型驱动的分裂规则（基于同步潜在预算模型）生成树结构，并提供了可视化解释、剪枝和决策树选择等工具。在肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）疾病进展的性别差异研究中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可解释人工智能时代，单一决策树因其易解释性而受到重新关注。然而，标准的树生长方法无法优化条件分裂规则，特别是在存在分层因子（如时间、空间或人口统计学变量）作为控制变量或潜在混杂因素时。现有方法缺乏一种能够在控制分层因子影响的同时进行树分裂的模型驱动方法。

Method: 提出SLBT框架，核心是基于同步潜在预算模型（SLBM）及其约束版本的模型驱动分裂规则：子节点被解释为父节点的同步混合模型的潜在成分，混合参数控制不同组别的观测分配到子节点，潜在预算参数更新控制变量各水平下的响应类别分布。参数通过最小二乘法估计，从神经网络视角进行建模。提供可视化解释工具、剪枝和决策树选择过程，并设计了处理不平衡响应类别分布的指标。

Result: 该方法成功应用于肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）疾病进展的性别差异研究，验证了SLBT框架在揭示分层因子（性别）影响下疾病进展模式差异的有效性。提供了SLBT库及相关树算法（GitHub开源）。

Conclusion: SLBT为存在分层因子的分类问题提供了一种可解释的概率机器学习方法，能够同时建模分层变量的影响并生成易于理解的树结构。该方法在解释性和性能之间取得了良好平衡，尤其适用于需要控制混杂因素的医学和社会科学领域。

Abstract: In the era of Explainable Artificial Intelligence, there is a renewed focus on single trees for their ease of interpretation. This paper introduces Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees, a probabilistic machine learning framework for classification trees in the presence of a stratification factor such as a temporal, spatial, or demographic variable, acting as a control variable or potential confounder. Standard tree growth procedures are not designed to optimize a conditional split rule. A model-based split rule is proposed in which child nodes are interpreted as latent components of a simultaneous mixture model, such as the Simultaneous Latent Budget Model and its constrained versions, fitted to the parent node. Mixing parameters drive the observations, differently for each group, to the child nodes whereas latent budgets parameters update the response classes profile of each level of the control variable. Parameters are estimated by least squares considering a neural network perspective of the model. An informative tree structure can be interactively visualized with interpretation aids on the node and the paths, including visual pruning and decision tree selection procedure. Suitable measures are proposed to handle an unbalanced response class distribution. The proposed methodology is applied to investigate gender-related differences in disease progression of Amyotrophic Lateral Sclerosis. The SLBT library with the various tree-based algorithms is available in the linked GitHub repository.

</details>


### [129] [Majority-of-Three is Optimal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13614)
*Divit Rawal,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了在可实现PAC学习框架下，三个独立一致分类器的多数投票是最优学习器，简化了先前投票学习器的算法与概率分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明集成方法（如投票）在PAC学习中具有优势，但现有最优投票方案（如Hanneke算法、Green Larsen的Bagging分析）结构复杂，需要更简洁的算法与证明。

Method: 通过严密的概率论推导，对三个独立一致分类器的多数投票机制进行最优性证明，简化了此前S. Hanneke和K. Green Larsen的工作。

Result: 证明了三个独立一致分类器的多数投票在可实现PAC学习环境下是最优的，且该方案比以往投票学习器更为简单。

Conclusion: 最简单的投票方案（3个分类器）即可达到最优学习效果，为集成学习和PAC学习的最优算法设计提供了更简洁的理论基础。

Abstract: We give a short proof that the majority vote of three independent consistent classifiers is an optimal learner in the realizable PAC setting. This proves optimality for the simplest voting scheme, while simplifying both the algorithmic structure and the probabilistic analysis of previous voting learners, including the algorithm of S. Hanneke and the analysis of bagging by K. Green Larsen.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [SAIGuard: Communication-State Simulation for Proactive Defense of LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12474)
*Ruxue Shi,Yili Wang,Mengnan Du,Qinggang Zhang,Rui Miao,Yixin Liu,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出SAIGuard，一种基于通信状态仿真的主动防御框架，通过检测和净化可疑消息来防御多智能体系统的安全威胁，在降低攻击成功率的同时保持系统效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS防御主要采用事后检测隔离的反应式范式，可能导致不可逆损害并降低协作效用，亟需主动防御方法。

Method: SAIGuard通过MAS交互图进行通信状态仿真，估计输入消息对局部智能体状态和全局MAS状态的影响，利用与良性通信模式的重构偏差检测风险消息，并对可疑消息进行净化或重生成。

Result: 在多种拓扑结构和攻击场景下，SAIGuard降低了攻击成功率，同时保持了MAS的协作效用，优于反应式防御方法。

Conclusion: 主动防御范式比反应式防御更有效，SAIGuard为MAS安全提供了一种不牺牲协作效用的可行方案。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) solve complex tasks through inter-agent collaboration, but their communication-driven nature also allows security risks to spread across agents and trigger system-wide failures. Existing MAS defenses mainly follow a reactive paradigm after execution by detecting and isolating harmful agents, which may cause irreversible damage and degrade collaborative utility. To address this, we propose a proactive defense framework for MAS security, namely a Simulation-aware Interception Guard (SAIGuard). SAIGuard performs communication-state simulation over the MAS interaction graph, estimates the impact of incoming messages on local agent states and the global MAS state, and detects risky messages via reconstruction deviations from benign communication patterns. Instead of isolating agents, SAIGuard sanitizes or regenerates suspicious messages before it propagation into system. Experiments across diverse topologies and attack scenarios show that SAIGuard reduces attack success rates while maintaining MAS utility, outperforming reactive defenses.

</details>


### [131] [Smarter Saboteurs, Better Fixers: Scaling & Security in Linear Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12709)
*Timothy McAllister,Sina Abdidizaji,Ivan Garibay,Ozlem Ozmen Garibay*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于LLM的多智能体系统中，模型规模对线性协作结构安全性的影响，发现大模型更容易执行恶意指令（性能下降达53.7个百分点），但通过轻量级终端修复阶段可将损失降至0.6个百分点，证明线性结构在适当纠正下具有抗攻击韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM多智能体系统在实际场景中部署，其协作结构在面对对抗性攻击（如提示注入、越狱）时的韧性成为关键安全问题。现有研究对模型规模与系统级安全性之间的相互作用理解不足。

Method: 在HumanEval基准上，对两个开源模型家族的不同规模版本进行实验，构建线性多智能体工作流，测试模型在有无终端修复阶段情况下执行恶意指令的倾向性。

Result: 发现了"服从-纠正对称性"：大模型更忠实地执行恶意指令（27B模型性能下降53.7pp）；但添加轻量级终端Fixer阶段后，性能损失降至0.6pp，恢复统计上与对照组相当的水平。

Conclusion: 严格线性协作结构在加入纠正机制后可以具备可行性和抗攻击韧性；以往研究中归因于线性拓扑的脆弱性可能源于缺乏纠正环节，而非结构本身的问题。

Abstract: As LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) are deployed in the wild, the resilience of their collaboration structures against adversarial compromise becomes a critical safety concern. Attackers may leverage prompt-injection or jailbreaking to sabotage individual agents within MAS workflows, but the interaction between model scaling and system-level resilience remains poorly understood. This paper investigates how model scale affects the security of linear multi-agent workflows. Our experiments across scales of two open-weight model families on the HumanEval benchmark reveal a compliance-correction symmetry: larger models are far more likely to faithfully execute malicious instructions, with the control-to-malicious performance drop reaching 53.7pp at 27B in uncorrected pipelines. However, appending a lightweight terminal Fixer stage collapses this to 0.6pp and restores statistical parity with control-level performance, demonstrating that strictly linear collaboration structures can be viable and resilient to adversaries at this scale, and suggesting that the brittleness previously attributed to linear topology may stem from a lack of correction.

</details>


### [132] [The Internet of Agentic AI: Communication, Coordination, and Collective Intelligence at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12835)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了“物联网智能体”（Internet of Agentic AI, IoAI）的愿景，一个异构自主AI智能体能够在云、边缘、设备、组织和物理-数字环境中相互发现、协商、通信、调用工具并执行工作流的开放生态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主AI智能体的快速涌现，人工智能正从孤立的模型推理转向分布式推理、通信与行动系统。现有架构缺乏支持大规模异构智能体间互操作、协调和信任的通用框架，亟需对这一新兴范式进行系统化研究。

Method: 综合单智能体AI、多智能体系统、分布式计算、通信网络、博弈论和安全工程等多学科基础，提出IoAI架构框架，并通过自适应制造和分布式操作协调两个案例进行验证。

Result: 形成了涵盖智能体部署模型、工作流生命周期、通信协议、互操作层、资源管理挑战和信任架构的完整框架，识别出受控涌现、语义互操作性、安全身份、激励相容协调、资源感知编排和治理等核心研究挑战。

Conclusion: IoAI为大规模自主智能体网络的架构设计提供了系统性蓝图，明确了未来在受控涌现、安全治理和资源优化等方面的关键研究方向。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of autonomous AI agents is transforming artificial intelligence from isolated model inference into distributed systems of reasoning, communication, and action. This paper develops the vision of the Internet of Agentic AI (IoAI): an open ecosystem in which heterogeneous agents discover one another, negotiate responsibilities, exchange context, invoke tools, and execute workflows across cloud, edge, device, organizational, and cyber-physical environments. We synthesize foundations from single-agent agentic AI, multi-agent systems, distributed computing, communication networks, game theory, and security engineering to characterize the architectures and mechanisms required for scalable agent ecosystems. The paper examines agent deployment models, workflow lifecycles, communication protocols, interoperability layers, resource-management challenges, and trust architectures, with case studies in adaptive manufacturing and distributed operational coordination. The resulting framework highlights the central research challenges of controlled emergence, semantic interoperability, secure identity, incentive-compatible coordination, resource-aware orchestration, and governance for large-scale networks of autonomous agents.

</details>


### [133] [Effects of Social Interactions in Self-Organising Railway Traffic Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13068)
*Fabio Oddi,Federico Naldini,Leo D'Amato,Grégory Marlière,Paola Pellegrini,Vito Trianni*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究自组织铁路交通管理中预测邻域视界（predictive neighbourhood horizon）这一结构参数，发现短视界足以满足需求，长视界反而降低局部可解性和计算响应速度，且无益于全局调度最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自组织交通管理面临扩展至复杂现实网络的挑战，其中列车需要预测邻域并协商达成调度共识。预测邻域视界作为关键设计变量，直接影响局部交互拓扑和问题复杂度，但其对分布式协调过程的影响尚不明确，需要系统研究其带来的权衡。

Method: 通过闭环仿真框架，系统评估不同预测邻域视界对分布式协调全过程的影响，从初始冲突检测到分布式调度共识达成，分析视界选择在局部可解性、计算响应速度与全局调度一致性和可行性之间的权衡。

Result: 与直觉相反，短时间视界即可满足需求；长视界不仅损害局部可解性和计算响应速度，而且在全局调度最优性方面没有任何收益。

Conclusion: 在自组织铁路交通管理中，采用较短的预测邻域视界更为合理，可避免长视界带来的计算负担而无明显性能损失。

Abstract: Recent research is exploring self-organised traffic management as a solution for scaling to complex real-world networks. In such a system, trains predict their neighbourhood, produce traffic plan hypotheses, and agree via consensus with neighbours on a future traffic plan to be implemented. This paper investigates a structural parameter within this pipeline: the predictive neighbourhood horizon. The horizon is used by trains to identify future potential conflicts with neighbours, and to establish the local interaction topology, that is, the subset of trains to negotiate with. As the primary design variable, the horizon directly determines the size and density of the social interaction graph, whereas its impact on the complexity of local sub-problems and the distributed consensus dynamics represents a trade-off to be explored. Through a closed-loop simulation framework the study evaluates how variations of the horizon impact the overall decentralised coordination process, from initial conflict detection to distributed schedule consensus. The analysis focuses on investigating the potential trade-off introduced by the horizon choice: balancing local tractability and computational responsiveness with the need for global schedule coherence and feasibility in safety-critical environments. Contrary to intuition, our empirical results indicate that the short time horizons suffice, while long values compromise local tractability and computational responsiveness with no gain in global schedule optimality.

</details>


### [134] [$α$-fair heterogeneous agent reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13076)
*Yao-hua Franck Xu,Tayeb Lemlouma,Jean-Marie Bonnin,Arnaud Braud*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种将α-公平性与异构智能体信任域学习（HATRL）结合的新框架，通过公平优势函数动态加权智能体效用，在保证单调改进和纳什均衡收敛的同时，实现了从功利主义效率到α-公平福利的平滑过渡。实验表明，所提算法（α-公平HATRPO和α-公平HAPPO）在功利主义视角下优于原HATRL算法，并取得了更高的社会性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统中，功利主义目标虽能最大化整体效率但忽视奖励分配，导致不公平的“领导者-跟随者”动态；而基于公平性的方法又常破坏马尔可夫博弈的平稳性或缺乏理论保证，存在公平目标与安全学习框架之间的关键缺口。

Method: 提出将α-公平性与异构智能体信任域学习（HATRL）结合的框架，设计公平优势函数，根据智能体的期望回报动态加权其效用，使全局目标能通过参数α从纯功利主义效率平滑过渡到α-公平福利。并据此提出两种实用算法：α-公平HATRPO和α-公平HAPPO。

Result: 在CleanUp和CommonHarvest等顺序社交困境实验环境中，所提算法在功利主义视角下表现优于原HATRL算法，同时实现了更高的社会性结果。

Conclusion: 所提框架成功弥合了公平目标与理论安全学习之间的差距，在保证单调改进和纳什均衡收敛的理论性质下，有效改善了多智能体合作中的公平性表现。

Abstract: Cooperation in multi-agent systems is typically optimized through utilitarian objectives that maximize overall efficiency but fail to account for reward distribution, often resulting in inequitable "leader-follower" dynamics. While fairness-based approaches encourage pro-social behaviors where every agent benefits from cooperation, many current algorithms - including those utilizing reward shaping - break the stationarity of Markov Games or lack rigorous theoretical guarantees. This creates a critical gap between fair objective methods and theoretically safe learning frameworks. We propose a novel framework that bridges $α$-fairness with Heterogeneous-Agent Trust Region Learning (HATRL), ensuring monotonic improvement and convergence toward Nash Equilibria. Our approach leverages a fair advantage function that dynamically weights agent utilities based on their expected returns, allowing the global objective to transition from purely utilitarian efficiency to $α$-fairness welfare based on the parameter $α$. We introduce two practical algorithms, $α$-fair HATRPO and $α$-fair HAPPO, and demonstrate through experiments in sequential social dilemmas like CleanUp and CommonHarvest that they perform better than HATRL's algorithms from a utilitarian point of view while achieving socially higher outcomes.

</details>


### [135] [See What I See, Know What I Think: Dense Latent Communication Across Heterogeneous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13594)
*Siyi Chen,Xiaoyan Zhang,Meng Wu,Jonathan Tremblay,Valts Blukis,Stan Birchfield,Rene Vidal,Alvaro Velasquez,Sijia Liu,Qing Qu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出一种用于异构多智能体系统的KV-cache通信方法，通过轻量级跨模型缓存转换和两阶段训练（重建+生成），实现跨模型潜空间对齐，在6个方向上优于基线，计算成本降低2-3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统依赖文本通信，存在高延迟和高计算成本；已有KV-cache通信方法局限于同构模型（相同模型副本），或异构方法限制较多（需共享输入、仅用于引导）。作者希望探索异构智能体能否实现真正的"读心"——既传递"看到什么"（感知），也传递"如何思考"（推理过程）。

Method: 信息结构分析揭示了对偶性：上下文感知传输由稀疏推理信号驱动，上下文无关传输需密集上下文知识保留。提出轻量级跨模型缓存转换器，采用两阶段训练：第一阶段重建（对齐潜空间），第二阶段生成（优化生成质量）。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B/8B/14B六个方向及六个域内/域外基准测试中，方法优于异构基线；上下文感知设置下匹配或超越文本通信，计算成本降低约2-3倍；上下文无关传输中有效，而基线方法完全失效。

Conclusion: 异构模型可以通过精心设计的对齐方法实现高效的KV-cache通信，在减少计算开销的同时保持甚至超越文本通信的质量，为多智能体系统的高效协作提供了新途径。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems communicate mostly through text, paying a lossy and expensive decode and re-encode cost. KV-cache communication is a promising alternative, yet most prior work is homogeneous, using duplicate copies of the same model, and avoids the central challenge of cross-model latent alignment; existing heterogeneous methods are also restrictive, typically assuming shared input and using transferred caches mainly for steering. We study a more fundamental question: can heterogeneous agents be aligned well enough to perform real "mind reading" and transfer both what one agent sees and how it thinks? Our information-structure analysis reveals a duality: context-aware transfer is driven by sparse reasoning signals, while context-unaware transfer, where the receiver sees no input, requires dense contextual knowledge preservation. Motivated by this, we propose dense alignment for heterogeneous KV-cache communication via a lightweight cross-model cache transformation and two-phase training: reconstruction followed by generation. Across all six directions of {Qwen3-4B, 8B, 14B} and six in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, our method outperforms prior heterogeneous baselines, matches or exceeds text communication in context-aware settings at roughly 2 to 3 times lower compute, and remains effective in context-unaware transfer where prior methods collapse.

</details>


### [136] [Tuning Agent-Based Predator-Prey Models Toward Lotka-Volterra Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13639)
*Corinna Mandl,Siddharth Chaturvedi,Marcel van Gerven*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文使用基于JAX的ABMax框架构建连续捕食者-猎物模型（绵羊和狼作为具有RNN控制器的智能体），通过特征损失函数优化环境参数，使群体动态呈现经典的Lotka-Volterra周期振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模智能体模型中的局部规则和参数微小变化易导致失控行为、种群崩溃或边界饱和，需要有效的参数调优方法来产生稳定的周期动态。

Method: 在ABMax框架中构建包含局部感知、内部能量和RNN控制器的连续捕食者-猎物系统；设计基于特征的损失函数（奖励持续振荡、相位滞后、种群有界和长期存续）；先对随机控制器进行参数优化，再进化控制器后在更自然场景中优化。

Result: 通过特征损失函数成功调优环境参数，使具有RNN控制器的智能体种群产生类似Lotka-Volterra的周期振荡动态。

Conclusion: 基于特征损失的方法能够有效优化复杂智能体模型的参数，使涌现的群体行为与经典生态动力学模型一致。

Abstract: Recent growth in compute power has made it increasingly feasible to use large-scale agent-based models to simulate complex adaptive systems. A central difficulty is that such models contain many local rules and parameters, where small changes can lead to runaway behaviour, population collapse, or saturation at artificial bounds. We study this problem in a continuous predator-prey system where sheep and wolves are active agents with local sensing, internal energy, and recurrent neural network-based controllers. We ask whether environmental and demographic parameters can be tuned so that the resulting population dynamics resemble classical Lotka-Volterra cycles. We optimise these parameters with a feature-based loss that rewards sustained oscillations, phase lag, bounded populations, and long-term persistence, first for random controllers and then for evolved controllers in a more naturalistic setting. The model is implemented in ABMax, a JAX-based agent-based modelling framework that enables efficient batched simulation on hardware accelerators.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [ToolSense: A Diagnostic Framework for Auditing Parametric Tool Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12451)
*Ashutosh Hathidara,Sai Shruthi Sistla,Sebastian Schreiber,Sahil Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolSense诊断框架，揭示基于参数化工具检索的LLM存在"知识-检索分离"现象：在模糊查询下性能大幅下降（最多64个百分点），且检索强未必理解工具功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具检索基准使用完整明确的查询，且评估时限制解码输出有效路径，无法反映模型是否真正理解工具语义。需要更真实的诊断框架来揭示模型对工具的实际理解程度。

Method: 提出ToolSense开源诊断框架，自动生成三种基准：1）真实检索基准（RRB），含三种模糊程度的查询；2）多选题探测基准；3）问答探测基准。应用于ToolBench约47k工具，评估五种参数化模型训练配置。

Result: 在RRB模糊查询下，多个配置性能大幅下降50-64个百分点，低于嵌入模型基线。部分模型虽检索强，但事实探测得分近乎随机，表明存在知识-检索分离。

Conclusion: 当前参数化工具检索方法在真实模糊场景下表现脆弱，高检索性能不等于真正的工具理解，亟需更全面的评估框架。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as agents over large tool catalogs face a critical tool-retrieval bottleneck. As embedding-based retrieval approaches rely on compact encoders that may under-capture specialized tool semantics, parametric tool retrieval addresses this by encoding each tool as a virtual token appended to the LLM vocabulary, fine-tuned in two stages (memorization then retrieval SFT) to use the LLM as a retriever, achieving strong performance on standard ToolBench retrieval benchmarks. Yet these benchmarks use verbose, fully-specified queries, and their evaluation applies constrained decoding that restricts outputs to valid token paths, neither reveals whether the model actually understands its tools. We introduce \textbf{ToolSense}, an open-source LLM-powered diagnostic framework that takes any tool catalog as input and automatically generates three benchmarks: a Realistic Retrieval Benchmark (RRB) with queries at three ambiguity tiers, an MCQ probing benchmark, and a QA probing benchmark. Applying ToolSense to ToolBench (~47k tools) and evaluating five parametric model training configurations reveals a knowledge-retrieval dissociation: on RRB queries, several configurations collapse by ~50-64 percentage points compared to fully-specified ToolBench benchmarks, falling below the embedding-model baseline. Additionally, despite strong retrieval performance, some models score near-random on factual probes, suggesting a knowledge-retrieval dissociation. We open-source the ToolSense framework and the ToolBench diagnostic benchmarks at https://github.com/SAP/toolsense.

</details>


### [138] [Arbor: Tree Search as a Cognition Layer for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12563)
*Neha Prakriya,Chaojun Hou,Zheng Gong,Huasha Zhao,Xi Zhao,Mou Li,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Arbor是一个多智能体框架，通过结构化树搜索作为认知层，在大型有状态动作空间中实现自主优化。在LLM推理优化任务中，相比供应商优化基线实现高达193%的吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进，且具有硬件无关性和可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主优化系统只能处理孤立目标且无状态评估，缺乏对大规模、有状态动作空间的有效探索能力。全栈LLM推理优化需要跨应用、框架、编译器、内核和硬件栈的协调努力，传统方法无法自主完成。

Method: 提出Arbor多智能体框架，核心是显式维护一个评分假设的搜索树作为共享工作记忆。包含Orchestrator（委托领域专家）和Critic（根因分析、内省、测量验证）两个智能体形成制衡架构。将智能体能力分解为硬技能（领域专长）和软技能（协调协议）。

Result: 在LLM推理优化中实现高达193%的吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进，而单智能体仅达+33%且数小时内崩溃。跨多代硬件平台泛化，运行间方差在2个百分点以内。

Conclusion: Arbor通过结构化搜索树记忆和智能体制衡架构，实现了全栈自主优化，方法具有硬件无关性和可重复性，显著优于单智能体方案和供应商优化基线。

Abstract: Arbor is a multi-agent framework that introduces structured tree search as a cognition layer for autonomous agents operating in large, stateful action spaces. Prior autonomous optimization systems operate on isolated targets with stateless evaluation. Arbor instead maintains an explicit search tree of scored hypotheses that serves as the shared working memory across agents, evolving with every measurement, treating failures as diagnostic signal that reshapes subsequent exploration, and expanding as prior successes shift the bottleneck distribution.
  We validate Arbor on full-stack LLM inference optimization, a domain where achieving peak performance has historically required coordinated effort from engineering teams across the application, framework, compiler, kernel, and hardware stack. Arbor pairs an Orchestrator agent, which drives optimization by delegating to Domain Specialists across the inference stack, with a Critic agent that safeguards stability through root-cause analysis, introspection, and measurement validation -- a checks-and-balances architecture where neither agent can unilaterally drive the system. Agent capabilities are decomposed into hard skills (domain expertise) and soft skills (coordination protocols that determine how contributions compose), enabling fully autonomous multi-day campaigns. Arbor achieves up to 193% inference throughput-latency Pareto improvement over vendor-optimized baselines, while a single agent without the harness plateaus at +33% throughput improvement and crashes irrecoverably within hours. Arbor generalizes to multiple generations of hardware platform, and run-to-run variance is within 2 percentage points demonstrating that the method is hardware-agnostic and reproducible.

</details>


### [139] [Strategic Decision Support for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12587)
*Shayan Kiyani,Sima Noorani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种战略决策支持框架，通过优化问题最小化支持使用量，同时控制反事实错过支持错误率，使AI代理在行动时保持与人类目标和约束的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策支持研究人类如何利用机器学习模型做决策，但在现代智能体系统中角色反转——AI代理代表用户行动，人类和工具成为支持机制。这种反转带来了可靠性问题，因为代理错误可能造成严重后果，且代理行为必须与人类目标和约束保持一致。

Method: 通过一个优化问题建立框架，最小化支持使用量，同时控制反事实错过支持错误（即代理独自行动时本应通过支持改善输出的概率）。在总体层面证明最优策略是支持价值的阈值规则，并开发了一种在线自适应阈值算法，结合随机探索以无分布假设方式控制错过支持错误，还引入了在线校准方法减少不必要的支持调用。

Result: 在信息收集、人机协作和工具使用等多种场景下的实验表明，该方法能够可靠地控制目标错误率，同时在实际中大幅减少支持使用量。

Conclusion: 该框架将传统决策支持的两个基本原则（支持的代价-价值权衡和不确定性量化的作用）重新应用于AI代理作为核心行动者的设定中，为智能体系统的可靠性保障提供了新的理论视角和实用算法。

Abstract: Traditionally, decision support studies how humans use machine learning models to make better decisions. In modern agentic systems, this division of roles is increasingly reversed: AI agents act on behalf of users, while humans and tools becomes support mechanisms around them. This role reversal brings reliability concerns to the forefront, since agentic errors can be consequential and agent behavior must remain aligned with human goals and constraints. Departing from the classical view of decision support, we revisit its two basic principles, the cost--value tradeoff of seeking support and the role of uncertainty quantification, in a setting where AI agents are the central actors. We propose a framework for strategic decision support for AI agents through an optimization problem that minimizes support usage subject to controlling a counterfactual missed-support error: the probability that the agent acts alone on instances where support would have materially improved its output. At the population level, we show that the optimal policy is a threshold rule on the value of support. Building on this structure, we develop an online algorithm that adaptively thresholds such a score and uses randomized exploration to control missed-support error without distributional assumptions. We further introduce a calibration-on-the-fly method that reduces unnecessary support calls online. We instantiate this framework across diverse scenarios, including information gathering, human--AI collaboration, and tool use, showing how each can be modeled through the same strategic decision-support lens. Experiments across these settings show that our method reliably controls the target error while substantially reducing support usage in practice.

</details>


### [140] [Pythagoras-Prover: Advancing Efficient Formal Proving via Augmented Lean Formalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12594)
*Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Zheng Zhao,Mihaela Cătălina Stoian,Qiyuan Xu,Haonan Li,Wenda Li,Shay B. Cohen,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Pythagoras-Prover系列，包括4B和32B自回归模型及首个基于扩散的4B证明器，通过课程SFT、动态推理过滤和ALF数据增强技术，在极小计算预算下达到甚至超越千亿参数模型的定理证明性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Lean定理证明器依赖大量训练和推理计算，原因是形式证明数据稀缺且证明搜索轨迹长，导致监督微调和采样成本高昂，亟需在实用计算预算下高效的证明方案。

Method: 1) 构建按难易程度分级的Lean验证语料进行课程SFT；2) 动态证明推理过滤方案将每个实例控制在8k token内；3) 提出增强型Lean形式化（ALF），通过扰动已知问题自我蒸馏扩充训练信号，无需逐一验证每个变异实例。

Result: Pythagoras-Prover-4B以~167倍更少的参数在MiniF2F-Test上超越DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B（86.1% vs 82.4%）；32B模型达93.0%开源最优，并在PutnamBench上解决93道题。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的训练策略和数据增强，小参数模型可在形式定理证明任务上超越大模型，为高效证明研究提供了新的开源基准和方法论。

Abstract: Modern Lean theorem provers achieve strong performance only with substantial training and inference compute, driven in part by scarce verified proof data and the long reasoning traces of formal proof search, making both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and sampling expensive. We introduce Pythagoras-Prover, a compute-efficient open-source family of Lean theorem provers built for practical compute budgets.
